  - **Don't** put the variant in the injected agent config: it is fixed at process start.
  - **The combined value is OpenCode syntax.** Restrict it to OpenCode before another ACP provider is added.
- **How it was found:**
  - The shim `scratchpad\shim\opencode.cmd`, put first on a throwaway daemon's `PATH`, logs every ACP line.
  - It only reproduced with instructions on and new conversations sent back to back (`mcp_smoke.py` with `SMOKE_INSTR=1 SMOKE_AGAIN=3`).
  - Single-bot tests with no instructions never hit it.
- **OpenCode 2's `question` tool needs ACP `elicitation`, which Retinue doesn't advertise, so it always failed.**
  - **Config:** the injected config sets `"permission": {"question": "deny"}`. A probe showed it wins over the build agent's built-in allow, and the tool is no longer offered.
  - **Cards:** any `question` tool card that still arrives is dropped (`hides_question_card`).
  - **Later:** supporting elicitation as a Retinue question card.
- **Tests:** the fake peer's `--stale-effort N` reproduces the race. When effort goes through the model option, tests assert `model=<m>/<effort>`, not `effort=<e>`.
- **The MCP explains failures:** `wait_for_turn`, `turn_metrics` and `read_conversation` carry the turn's error (`external::turn_error`, secret-marker detail hidden).

## 2026-10-29 · Daemon and UI · Wave 14 (the Retinue MCP)

- **The debug daemon ran out of worker stack after the MCP work.** The dev app and the standalone daemon crashed on `daemon.shutdown` (and, before the route was boxed, on the first request) with "thread 'tokio-rt-worker' has overflowed its stack", exit `0xC00000FD`. The beta.40 debug build shut down cleanly, and the same build ran fine with `RUST_MIN_STACK=4194304`, so the code was correct but deeper prompt-path futures pushed debug frames past tokio's 2 MiB default. Boxing single arms (`external::rpc`, `queue_rpc`, `session_steer`) did not help. Fix: `main()` builds the runtime itself with `thread_stack_size(WORKER_STACK_BYTES)` (8 MiB), and the `ext.*` route and the queue's four prompt calls are `Box::pin`ned. To avoid it: after any change to the prompt path, run a standalone debug daemon through `daemon.health` and `daemon.shutdown` and check the exit code is 0. The cargo test gate cannot catch this, because it runs with `RUST_MIN_STACK=33554432`.
- **A standalone daemon is reachable only from PowerShell here.** From the Bash tool, loopback HTTP to the daemon was reset; the same Python script worked from PowerShell. Use `scratchpad` scripts with `urllib` plus `ProxyHandler({})` and run them through PowerShell.
- **A new route prefix in `main_rpc` needs a family row in `tiers.json`.** The TS parity test (`remoteTiers.parity.test.ts`) wants a family rule for every `starts_with("…")` guard, even for methods outside `dispatch`. `ext.*` is `never`.
- **An outside client can never claim an origin.** `meta.origin` is written only by `PromptOrigin::Mcp`; `sanitize_prompt_meta` returns `None` for `User`. Queued MCP items carry `meta.kind:"mcp"` so the drain keeps the mark. A steer that comes back stale and is queued loses the mark (`enqueue_steer` stores only text).
- **A long-lived stdio server must not run from the install folder.** The installed daemon runs from a private copy, and a client holding `retinued.exe` open would block updates. `external.rs` copies the executable to `<data>\retinue-mcp.exe` (a `.new` file, then rename; a locked target keeps the old copy). The bridge re-reads the endpoint file on a 401 or a connect failure, so a rotated token or a restarted daemon needs no client restart.
- **Tests that copy the executable must not copy the test binary:** `bridge_source()` is `#[cfg(test)]`-swapped for a small file.
- **Build-folder paths turned into control characters.** Release entries written with Python inside a Bash heredoc lost the backslash in `D:\b40`: Bash turned a doubled backslash into a single one, and Python then read `\b` as a backspace byte. 28 backspaces had collected in `release-checklist.md`, `session-handoff.md`, `lessons.md` and `pipeline.md`. When a script writes text that contains Windows paths, put the script in a file with the Write tool. Afterwards, check that `grep -rlP "\x08" docs .claude/skills --include=*.md` prints nothing.
- **A "flaky" test was a real race (2026-11-01):** `a_crash_after_a_finished_turn_does_not_fail_that_session` failed about one run in six. After `turn/start` returned, `CodexAdapter::prompt` set `turn.completed = false`, which could overwrite a completion the reader task had just recorded, so a finished turn looked active and a later process crash failed it. `completed` is now reset only before the call. Read the code before calling a failure timing-sensitive.
- **A fake adapter that answers at once finishes the turn.** Use a hold-open adapter when a test needs a live turn.

## 2026-10-28 · Daemon and UI · Wave 13 (loop guards, schedule rules, limit handoff, bot templates, phone assignments, outside sessions)

- **The hop limit counts round trips, not nesting.** `team::after_turn` relays a reply with the side conversation's depth and the Manager's next `message_blob` adds one, so `MAX_DEPTH` (6) bounds how many sequential hand-offs one request can make. Lowering it cuts ordinary multi-step plans short; real loops are stopped by `waiting_upstream` (refuses a target already up the `originSessionId` chain) and `repeated_failure` (two identical recent failures) in `team.rs`.
- **"Did the run change anything?" is answered conservatively.** `Storage::session_acted_since` counts any file change and any tool whose kind is not on the read-only list (kinds differ per provider: Codex `commandExecution`, Claude `Read`/`Grep`, ACP `edit`/`execute`), so an unknown tool means "changed" and the run is not retried on its own.
- **Automation starts are claimed in memory** (`Automations::claim_start`, released on drop). The `automation_runs` single-flight index alone let "Run now" and the scheduler both open a conversation before one insert failed.
- **A backup coding agent is a handoff, not a model switch.** A conversation's runtime is fixed, so "Continue in another bot…" on a usage-limit notice reuses `session.handoff.draft` and the `HandoffDialog`, listing bots on another provider first; nothing is sent automatically.
- **Mounted tests: the Automations list is sorted by name**, so select a card by `.schedule-name`, not by position.
- **Test normalizers from the daemon's raw DTO, not from a hand-built record.** Wave 12 added `silent`/`doneGoal` to the delegation DTO and to `DelegationRecord`, but `normalizeDelegation` (`ui/delegations.ts`) dropped them, so "Done, nothing to report" never showed; the label test built the record by hand and passed. Fixed in Wave 13 with a test that feeds the wire shape (`workspace: {path, worktree}`).
- **A field that carries a host path must be handled in both remote redaction ports** (`remote/policy.rs` `field_policy` and `remote/remoteTiers.ts` `fieldPolicy`) and get a case in `remote/fixtures/policy-goldens.json`, which both languages run. `worktree` is dropped and `workspace.path` reduced since Wave 13; opening a method to phones also means updating the reviewed event list in `remoteTiers.parity.test.ts`.
- **Writing another program's settings:** `outside.rs` reads Claude Code's `settings.json`, refuses anything that is not a JSON object, backs it up beside itself, writes through a temp file and rename, and removes only entries whose command holds `retinue-hook` and `--outside`. `serde_json` (no `preserve_order`) rewrites the keys in sorted order; the consent dialog shows the exact new file.
- **UI details:** `SettingsRow`'s `detail` renders inside a `<small>`, so status paragraphs go after the row (`.settings-row-note`); `ProviderLogo` is a `span`, so style a pill's label by class, never `.pill span`; a long unbroken line in a `<pre>` widens a grid dialog unless the dialog has `grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr)`.
- **The installed daemon runs from a private copy** (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\com.retinue.desktop
untime-processes\retinued-<pid>-<uuid>.exe`), so anything that looks "beside the daemon" finds nothing in a real install. The Health check's hook row and outside watching both did; a live test that ran `target\debug\retinued.exe` directly hid it. The shell now passes `RETINUE_HOOK_BRIDGE` (the bridge beside the installed daemon) and `outside::hook_bridge_path` reads it first. Test install-shaped behaviour through the shell or the dev app, not a hand-started daemon.
- **Unread dots must follow new content, not `updatedAt`.** Warming a conversation (and evicting the oldest warm one) changes its state and bumps `updated_at`, so switching quickly left white dots on conversations with nothing new. `sessionSeen.attentionAt` uses the newest reply, notice or teammate message, and keeps `updatedAt` only for states that need the owner (approval, error, limit).
- **The daemon supervisor only worked in a live crash test after two fixes** (2026-10-28, found by force-ending the dev app's daemon five times). The window's `ensure_daemon` started the daemon whenever it reconnected, so it raced the backoff and overrode "gave up"; and the backoff counter reset on every successful restart, so the wait never grew. Now `ensure_daemon` refuses while the supervisor is `restarting` or `gave_up` (`supervisor_blocks_start`), and the wait is indexed by the crashes inside the two-minute window. To repeat the test: run the dev app from a short path and end the `retinued-<pid>-<uuid>.exe` under `%LOCALAPPDATA%\com.retinue.desktop.dev
untime-processes`.
- **The silence watchdog already existed** (`forward_events_with_timeouts` in `main.rs`: 45 s to first output, 5 min between outputs, paused while `waiting_permission`). Wave 13 only widened it while a tool is still running (`RUNNING_TOOL_FACTOR`, 6x, so a long build or install is not stopped) and made the stop say which agent and how long (`silence_message`). Check for an existing mechanism before building one from the plan.
- **Lazy snapshot:** `app.snapshot { lazy: true }` (the window only) sends the 15 newest conversations and any busy one in full, the rest as `partial: true` with their last three messages (`Storage::snapshot_with`, `session_detail_parts`). The window's background loader (`hydrationOrder`) loads partial ones, open ones first; `mergeHydratedSession` clears `partial`. The phone keeps the full snapshot.
- **Retiring a look leaves `:root[data-retinue="on"]` rules everywhere.** A scripted cleanup must parse selector lists, keep live selectors, drop a comment only when it belongs to a removed rule, and be checked for balanced `/* */` and braces afterwards: a comment containing a comma once came out unterminated and would have hidden the rest of `polish.css`.
- **Bash heredocs lose doubled backslashes** in this tool even with a quoted `'EOF'`, so Windows paths and escaped newlines in generated code came out wrong twice. Write such scripts with the Write tool (or build the backslash with `chr(92)`) and typecheck right after.

## 2026-10-28 · Daemon · Wave 12 (approval rules, durable team queue, cascaded stops, quiet replies, supervisor, Doctor)

- **The ledger finishes the assignment before `team.rs` relays.** `delegations::after_turn` calls `finish(..., "completed")` and only then returns `TurnEnd::Owed(row)`, so a second pass in `team::after_turn` can no longer change the row (a `(pass)` check there found it already `completed` and relayed anyway). A silent outcome has to be a ledger decision: `TurnEnd::Silent` is returned instead of `Owed`, and `delegation_mark_silent_complete` writes status and `silent=1` in one statement. **Avoid:** any new "the reply is different" rule belongs where the row is finished, not where the reply is composed.
- **Async recursion across modules needs one `Box::pin`.** `cancel_session_turn_matched` → `delegations::cancel_turn_group` → `cancel_row` → `cancel_session_turn_if_quiet` → `cancel_session_turn_matched` is an async cycle (E0733) even though the quiet path never recurses at run time. Boxing the `cancel_turn_group` future at the call site fixes the type. The cascade itself is one loop over a worklist of `(session, turn)` pairs, so the count it returns is the whole chain.
- **Every queue pop must delete its durable row.** The team queue (`team_queue`) is the truth and `st.team.queued` is a cache carrying each row's id; the drain removes the row right after `pop_front`, and the cancel helpers (`retain_queued`) remove theirs too. A pop that forgets it makes a restart re-deliver a brief that already went out.
- **`resolve_permission_policy` allowlist.** It accepts `policy:auto`, `policy:bypass`, `policy:automation` and now `policy:rule:*`; a new policy reason that is not added there makes `finish_permission_policy_reply` return `InvalidAgent`, which leaves the card `resolving` — it looks like the auto-answer silently failed.
- **`session.model.update` refuses while a turn is active** ("model cannot change while a turn is active"). A test that needs plan mode on a live turn must write the lock directly with `Storage::session_apply_model_change`.
- **File encodings differ per file.** `crates/retinue-storage/src/lib.rs` is valid UTF-8 with one NUL and LF: a `readFileSync(utf8)`/`writeFileSync` round trip is byte-identical (verify with `Buffer.from(s,'utf8').equals(b)`), and the Read tool refuses a window that covers the NUL, so read it with `node`/`grep -a`. `crates/retinue-daemon/src/main.rs` and `src-tauri/src/lib.rs` are CRLF: a multi-line scripted replacement must split CRLF to LF first, edit, and convert back, or it matches nothing. Shell `node -e` strips backslashes; write the script to a file.
- **Storage tests:** a new additive `ensure_*_schema` needs its own `#[test]` (the attribute is not inherited by an inserted function), and a fixture agent needs `upsert_runtime` first or `agent_create` fails `AgentNotFound`.
- **The Doctor's fixes are RPCs.** `doctor.run` only offers `runtime.refresh` and `runtime.auth.clear`, and every row's `detail` is one plain sentence; the test asserts the shape and that no detail carries a secret word. Free disk space was skipped: no crate in the workspace can read it without a new dependency.

## 2026-10-27 · Tooling · A "binary-safe" edit of storage `lib.rs` corrupted every non-ASCII character

- **Symptom:** after a scripted edit, `message_reaction_rpc_persists…` failed with "Reaction or message role is unsupported" and `tool_details_are_bounded…` overran its limit. Every emoji and `…` in `crates/retinue-storage/src/lib.rs` had become mojibake.
- **Cause:** the Node helper read the file as `latin1` (to keep the NUL bytes) but wrote it with `fs.writeFileSync(file, text)`, which encodes as UTF-8. Each byte above 127 became two bytes.
- **Fix:** read the damaged file as UTF-8 and write it back with `Buffer.from(text, 'latin1')`. That only works when no character is above U+00FF and none is U+FFFD; check that first, then compare the diff and the NUL count (`tr -cd '\000' | wc -c`) with `HEAD`.
- **Avoid:** read and write the same encoding. Byte edits on this file need `latin1` on both sides (or `grep -a` plus `sed -i` on ASCII lines), and a diff check right after.

## 2026-10-27 · Looks and art · Frost, the 2D robot and pixel icons retired (owner)

- **What went:**
  - The Frost look, with its stylesheet, wallpapers and the wallpaper/glass settings UI.
  - The flat, 2D (`robot-pop`) and dot robot renderers.
  - The 16 pixel icons.

  The rules and migration are in `AGENTS.md` (27 October amendment) and `design-language.md`.
- **What a dropped value must not do:** every place that parses a look must map retired values, not just reject them. `normalizeLook` dropped an unknown `art`, so a saved `robot-pop` would have become Automatic (Glossy) instead of the 3D robot; it now maps `RETIRED_ROBOT_ARTS` to `robot` and `px-*` to `logo-tint`.
- **How the daemon handles them:** it keeps accepting the retired values (`RETIRED_ARTS`, `RETIRED_ICONS` in `valid_look`) and rewrites them in `look_column`, so old backups, pending proposals and older phones keep working.
- **Left in place:** the native wallpaper commands and their bridge exports (`selectWallpaperImage`, `importWallpaper`, `readWallpaper`, `clearWallpaper` in `tauri.ts`, its mirrors and `src-tauri/src/lib.rs`), plus the daemon's `appearance.wallpaper` and `appearance.glass` validation. They are unused now. They were left because those files belonged to the wave12 backend lane at the time; remove them once that lane merges.
- **Left in place:** `data-retinue="on"` selectors from Legacy still sit in several stylesheets. Nothing sets that marker; they are dead CSS.

## 2026-10-27 · Gates · Restoring the tests after the T3/T7 waves (Claude, main checkout, uncommitted)

The waves ran no tests; afterwards 141 TypeScript and 6 Rust tests failed. Three were real bugs, the rest pinned behaviour Codex changed on purpose.
- **A fresh-session model switch reported the old context use.** `update_session_model` built its summary inside `session_apply_model_change`, before emitting `session.context.reset`, so the reply and `session.changed` still carried `contextUsed`. Re-read `session_event_summary` after the reset (`main.rs`, after `session_apply_model_change`). **Avoid:** any summary sent after a reset must be read after the reset event is stored.
- **The phone bundle grew to 4.1 MB.** pdf.js (about 1.8 MB with its worker) was precached though `media.preview` is never remote; `vite.remote.config.ts` now aliases `./PdfViewer` to `src/remote/remotePdfViewer.tsx`. The Haze character SVGs (about 1.5 MB) are meant to be there, so `remoteBuild.test.ts` counts them on their own (under 2 MB) and keeps the shell under 1.5 MB. `remoteTauri.ts` also lacked `applyWindowBackdrop` and `windowBackdropState` (the parity test).
- **The rail asked the daemon about draft conversations.** `session.environment` (old), `session.workspace` and `session.checkpoints` (new) were sent with a `draft:` id; all three now skip drafts with `isDraftSession`. `WorkspaceBody`, `CheckpointsBody` and `ScheduleOverview` also tolerate a reply missing its list, since a crash while rendering takes the whole window down.
- **The model pill named an effort before a model was chosen** ("Pick a model · High effort" while the effort control was a placeholder); the effort is passed only with a model.
- **Tests that pinned old behaviour:** mounted App tests delivered daemon events only to the last registered listener (`daemonEventHandlers.at(-1)`), which became `useDelegations`' listener, so App never heard them; tests now broadcast to every live listener and the mock's unlisten removes it. Sends carry `clientMessageId` (not steers). Sent delegations render as an "Assigned to X" card (`.delegation-group`), not a chip. The busy composer shows a Steer now / After this turn toggle. The composer stays enabled while an approval waits. The model pill is `aria-disabled` while busy. Manager `message_blob` is sent with no approval card. Side conversations may `ask_user` unless their chain starts at an unattended run. A provider error with no watched turn fails only an in-flight session. A claimed queue item with a `sendId` and no message row restarts as `QUEUE_NOT_SENT_ERROR` (`ambiguous: false`). A follow-up to a busy teammate waits in the in-memory `st.team.queued`, not the database queue (lost on restart, as before).
- **Clippy:** `CancelTarget::Active` is boxed; `deliver_reply` and `repo_answer` take the crate's usual `too_many_arguments` allowance.

## 2026-10-27 · Native preview · Missing sherpa static library (uncommitted)

`D:\pv1` desktop development build could not find `sherpa-onnx-kaldifst-core.lib` because its `target/desktop-dev/sherpa-onnx-prebuilt` extraction was incomplete. A complete extraction from the matching `D:\hz1` target was copied into that preview target without removing the existing cache. A production-only daemon check had passed; this was a native build dependency issue. Do not delete or recurse through `node_modules` junctions while repairing a worktree.

## 2026-10-27 · Model switching, idempotent sends, assignments and restart truth (T7 wave, uncommitted, in `main`; no tests were run)
- **A model change always threw away the provider session, though all three adapters switch models in-session.** `update_session_model` cleared the provider id and demanded `confirmModelChange` for every provider, but Codex sends `model` in every `turn/start`, Claude's `prompt()` restarts with `--resume` and the new `--model` (effort changes already travel that way), and ACP/OpenCode selects the model config option before each prompt. Fix: `AgentAdapter::model_switch()` (`ModelSwitch::KeepsSession | NewSession`, default `NewSession`; opencode is `AcpAdapter`, so it inherits ACP) and `session.model.plan` (read-only; `continuity` kept / recap / none plus one provider-specific sentence). The composer asks the plan first and only confirms a `recap`. The lock write and the provider-id clear are one transaction (`session_apply_model_change`), and `expectedModel` refuses a stale choice. Not run against a live provider: the claim rests on reading those code paths. **Avoid:** never add a provider to `KeepsSession` without a `file:line` proof that its next prompt carries the model into the same provider session.
- **The composer's "model saved" could be an error.** `session.model.update` succeeded, then the bot-default write (`agent.update`) threw, and the catch reported a failure while the conversation had changed. The conversation result is shown first now and the default write is best effort with its own message.
- **Retry of an unclear send could duplicate a message.** `session.prompt` and `session.queue.add` accept `clientMessageId` (8 to 80 of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, stored in `meta.clientMessageId` / queue params, indexed); a replay returns `deduplicated:true`, different text under one id is `conflict`. The UI makes the id once per message (`newClientMessageId`, `busySend.ts`) and keeps it in the pending row, so Retry reuses it. `session.steer` is deliberately not idempotent.
- **Review caught a swallowed failure in the unmerged t6 patch.** Its top-of-`after_turn` guard returned `Waiting` whenever any other turn of the conversation was live, so a failing assignment A was dropped when assignment B's brief (or a retry) had just started, and the Manager never heard. Narrowed to "the live turn is the answer to this assignment's own question" (`answer_continues_row`). **Avoid:** a guard that suppresses a turn ending must name the exact turn it is waiting for.
- **Questions and assignments are keyed per assignment, never per conversation.** Native provider question cards are linked to their assignment and wake the Manager too (they had no wake at all); a card stuck `resolving` ages out (`resolvingAt`, 2 minutes); an answer that can no longer start a turn releases its hold; `retry_dropped` finds the row through every turn the assignment used (`turn_ids`); duplicate `message_blob` calls in one Manager turn are one assignment (`dedupe_key`, unique partial index); `queueReason` (`busy` or `slots`) says why a brief waits; cancel of a queued or ended assignment now relays its reply, and a restart that interrupts one leaves a content-free note. Both gaps noted here at first (wake lost across a restart, non-atomic cancel) were closed in round 2, below.
- **Boot recovery expired answerable cards and told nobody.** It expired every pending card, including `mcp:` questions and `proposal:` cards that are answered in the daemon, and emitted no event, so a live window kept a dead card. It now closes only native cards, emits `permission.resolved` with `reason:"daemon_restarted"` and writes one note per conversation. A session left `retrying` with no retry item goes to `idle`; automation sessions no longer get `continuation_pending`. A queued send that was `sending` at the crash is `failed` with `ambiguous:true`; resending or editing it needs `confirmResend:true`. Keep `recover_after_restart() -> Vec<String>` (a storage test compares it).
- **Hot reads.** Added `app_events(event_type,sequence)`, `messages(session_id,role)`, `projects(path)`, `permission_requests` on `delegationId` and `delegations(side_session_id,side_turn_id)`; `session_brief` replaces `session_detail` where only the link, provider or bot is read (queue calls, retry, `team::after_turn`). `app.snapshot` still ships every message of every conversation; that needs a lazy per-conversation load in the window.
- **Process.** The default `tsc -p apps/desktop` includes test files; for a no-tests rule use an untracked `apps/desktop/tsconfig.prod.tmp.json` (extends the real one, `types: ["node","vite/client"]`, excludes `*.test.ts(x)`) and remove it after. `crates/retinue-storage/src/lib.rs` contains NUL bytes: `grep -a`, edit it with a binary-safe script, and expect `git apply` to treat it as binary text. Combine lane patches in one worktree and `cargo check` there before touching the dirty main checkout (lane-only diff: `git diff <base.tree> <res.tree>`). One storage test is now stale on purpose (see `docs/history/t7/REPORT.md`).

- **Queued send recovery (2026-10-27):** each new queue item stores a stable `sendId` before delivery; restart reconciliation uses its message and turn evidence. Older claimed rows lacking `sendId` remain ambiguous, even if they contain a `clientMessageId`, because the old sender did not forward it. `send_now` reports send errors rather than claiming success; a changed failed message gets a fresh ID. No runtime verification was performed.

## 2026-10-27 · Round 2: durable wake, checked cancel, ambiguous sends, exhausted limits, pause (T7, uncommitted, in `main`; no tests were run)
- **The Manager wake is durable now.** `delegations.wake_state/wake_title/wake_kind` are written by the same conditional UPDATE that claims the wake; acked only after a real delivery (direct or the team-queue drain); `finish`, `question_resolved` and a skipped queued item drop it; boot (`offer_wake`) re-offers only for a running assignment, an existing non-archived Manager conversation and a still-pending card, deciding the died-before-ack case from the Manager's stored `blob_update` metadata by assignment and request IDs. The prompt path now persists that metadata; older messages without IDs may be re-offered after restart. **Avoid:** matching notification text conflates separate requests with identical titles.
- **Cancel by turn.** `cancel_session_turn_if(session, expected_turn)` checks the live turn inside the per-session gate (reservations need the same gate); `retry::cancel_pending` is session-wide, so it runs only after a match. Checked assignment cancellation holds the per-session gate while requesting the provider stop, with a bounded timeout; a timed-out stop remains unconfirmed. Native-provider timing and late interrupt handling remain unverified.
- **Delivery is unknown, not failed.** `session_prompt_impl` returns before the adapter is called (`run_turn` makes the one `adapter.prompt` call), so the only record is the turn's `data.delivery` (`acknowledged` from `PromptAccepted`, else `unknown`). A `clientMessageId` replay of a turn that ended unconfirmed returns `delivery_unknown` unless `confirmResend:true`; auto-retry declines such turns. The T7 round 1 replay used to answer `accepted` for a turn that had errored: that was wrong, fixed. Activation failures and cancels also read as unknown because nothing stored proves the adapter was not called.
- **Exhausted limits.** `watchdog_due_sessions` excludes `recovery_count>=3` and automation (the sweep ran a no-op query every second); `watchdog_settle_exhausted` is one conditional UPDATE to `idle` + `autoContinueExhausted`, spoken once by `retry::settle_exhausted`; `watchdog_reset` clears the flag.
- **Pause.** `delegation.paused` refuses only new Manager hand-offs (`team::delegate`); the owner-approved `assign_task` uses `delegate_approved` and is never blocked. **Avoid:** a pause or limit must never fail an approval the owner just gave.
- **Snapshot.** `app.snapshot` still returns every message; a summary mode needs the companion kept on full snapshots, a loaded-transcript carry-over in `refresh()` and `applyEvent` for unloaded sessions. See `docs/history/t7/REPORT-round2.md`.

## 2026-10-25 · Sessions, drafts, repository, dictation and Manager · Several FIX45 fixes (uncommitted, in `main`)
- **Stale Failed label (session outcome):** `session.state` can stay `error` after the bot recovers, when a late process error arrives with no active turn. Fix: a late error with no active turn cannot overwrite a completed outcome, and the newest completed turn in storage repairs a stale label. A genuine failure of an active turn stays failed. The UI presents state from the newest turn (`presentedSessionState`, `ui/sessionOutcome.ts`). Do not read assistant prose as success. The unproven ACP success inference is removed: `provider_turn_end` was eliminated, so a turn does not become success without protocol evidence that excludes failure and cancellation. Genuine provider errors remain failures.
- **Hold-to-delete jump:** a label that changes must share one grid cell sized by the longest label; the armed label must be shorter than the idle one (`Release to <first word>`); `transform: scale` on `.holding` moved the button. Holding, armed and pending keep the idle footprint (`ConfirmDialog.hold.mounted.test.tsx`). Not measured in a browser.
- **Drafts:** `ui/draftStore.ts` keeps text per conversation id and `draft:<agentId>` under `retinue.drafts.v1` (2 h since last edit on the local clock, 40 drafts). Capture the key before any `await`, give each held send its own id so an answer releases only its own hold, and never truncate the live text: only the restart backup is bounded (100,000 characters a draft, 1,000,000 in all).
- **Repository changes:** `session.repository` (`crates/retinue-daemon/src/repository.rs`, backend lane) reports Git's working tree against HEAD with shell-free, bounded probes, the real branch (or unborn or detached) and line totals. The rail labels it as the repository's state, not what this conversation wrote; conversation-recorded files stay in their own list. The method is read-only and **never remote** (`class: never` in `remote/tiers.json`), because raw diffs can contain environment values. Create PR is a separate message that the user confirms first.
- **Manager messages:** an ordinary message from Manager to a bot is sent directly again, with no task or proposal (the approval gate came from `fd738b2`, FIX39 P7). Consequential writes stay approved, and the archived-bot guard stays as it was.
- **Delegation lifecycle (research 2026-10-26; ledger built 2026-10-26, FIX47):** `team.rs` records side-session links and relays turn results; its team delivery queue and depth maps were in memory, and the WorkingBotsPanel lists side sessions globally, so a side session's state is not an assignment's state. FIX47 adds `crates/retinue-daemon/src/delegations.rs` with a durable `delegations` table (one row per `message_blob`/approved assignment: origin session and turn, side session and turn, workspace, worktree, status `queued|running|completed|failed|cancelled|interrupted`, `delivery_text` only while queued), `delegation.list`, the `delegation.updated` event, and restart reconciliation: a queued row is redelivered once, a running row takes its turn's own outcome or becomes `interrupted` (never left running). Two gotchas: `recover_after_restart` rewrites every live turn to `state='error'` with `data.watchdogInterrupted=true`, so use that marker to tell an interrupted brief from a provider failure; and `retry::after_failed_turn` records `turns.data.retryDecision` on the same terminal path as `team::after_turn`, in a different order per call site, so a failed delegation waits (bounded) for `retry` before it is reported failed. Still not durable exactly-once: an interrupted brief is never auto-redelivered. The separate task ledger has its own run records. T3 Code comparison: upstream 220eb735 uses parent-run delegation records, idempotent commands and completion delivery; verify runtime behaviour before claiming parity. **Window side (FIX47, 2026-10-26):** the Manager's conversation shows its assignments as one card per turn (`ui/DelegationGroup.tsx`, rows and status in `ui/delegations.ts`). Status is the daemon's recorded status, upgraded to "Needs approval" only while the teammate's conversation has a live pending permission (`isPendingPermissionLive`); a message sent before the ledger (no `delegationId` in the notice meta) gets the side conversation's state only when it is the newest to that conversation and is otherwise labelled "Earlier message", never a guessed status. Workspace and branch come from the record (`workspace.worktree` = the bot's own branch) or `session.repository` of the side conversation; a shared folder's branch and changes are labelled as the folder's, not the bot's. `delegation.list` is `class: never` in `remote/tiers.json` (paths are not redacted remotely) and `delegation.updated` is `drop`. Known limit: `started()` runs after the prompt is accepted, so a turn that ends before it would leave the row `running` until the next restart reconciles it. The first FIX47 pass was not type-checked or tested (owner said no tests); run the gates before release.
- **Recorder-only dictation (owner decision, not a bug):** the island shows no live words and no cloud note; the final transcript reaches the box on stop. See the FIX43 entry below.
- **Avoid:** do not add a second approval, draft or outcome rule beside these; read the module named above first, and test the stale case (a completed turn after a late error) and the hold footprint in the production build before calling them done.

## 2026-10-25 · Storage · `sqlite: no such table: tasks` on an existing database (FIX43, beta.37)
- **Symptom:** the installed beta.37 showed `sqlite: no such table: tasks` on the Tasks page; a fresh database worked.
- **Root cause:** `Storage::migrate` returns early in a branch per schema version (`ledger==12`, `==11`, `==10`, ...), and each branch calls its own list of `ensure_*_schema`. `ensure_tasks_schema` was added only to the last, fresh-database path (`crates/retinue-storage/src/lib.rs`), so every database an earlier version had created never got the task tables. The daemon tests all use `open_in_memory`, which takes the fresh path, so nothing caught it.
- **Fix:** `migrate` now wraps `migrate_versions` and calls `ensure_tasks_schema` once for every path. Test `an_existing_database_without_the_task_tables_gains_them_on_open` drops the task tables from a file database, reopens it and lists tasks (it fails without the fix).
- **Avoid:** a new additive `ensure_*_schema` goes in the shared wrapper, not in one version branch. Test an upgrade with a file database made by the previous shape, not only `open_in_memory`.

## 2026-10-25 · CSS · Message buttons sat above the bubble's middle (FIX43)
- **Symptom:** the time, copy, reply and reaction buttons beside a message were 7 px above the bubble's vertical centre (half of the 14 px row margin), more with a reaction badge.
- **Root cause:** `.message-action-shell` centres the buttons on its own box, but the `.message-row` inside it still had `margin-top: 14px` from `.message-row.user` / `.group-start` (`shell.css`) and 18 px from `:has(.message-reaction-badge)` (`experiments.css`). Those selectors tie with `.message-action-shell > .message-row { margin: 0 }`, and the later stylesheet wins.
- **Fix:** `.message-action-shell > .message-row:is(.user, .group-start, :has(.message-reaction-badge)) { margin: 0 }` (`experiments.css`); the shell owns the spacing. Measured 0 px in Haze, Frost, Stone and the companion; `messageActionsLayout.test.ts` pins the rule.
- **Avoid:** measure centring with `getBoundingClientRect` on the buttons and the bubble (`?experiments` turns the actions on in the preview); do not trust a screenshot.

## 2026-10-25 · UI · A `Select` inside a dialog grew the dialog (FIX43)
- **Symptom:** opening Project in the New task dialog drew the list inside the dialog and made it taller.
- **Root cause:** `.select-menu` is `position: absolute` under the trigger, so inside a scrolling, transformed dialog it extends the scroll area.
- **Fix:** `ui/Select.tsx` floats the list when its trigger sits inside `[role="dialog"]`: a portal into `document.body`, `position: fixed`, placed from the trigger's rect, flipped upwards when there is no room, re-placed on scroll and resize. Outside-click checks both the root and the floating list. Never use `visibility: hidden` for the first frame: it stops the list taking focus, so Escape would reach the dialog and close it.
- **Tests:** a Select in a dialog has its list outside the host. `ui/testSelect.ts` finds the list through the trigger's `aria-controls` (an older `trigger.parentElement.querySelector` fails for any Select in a dialog, as `MemoryPage.mounted.test.tsx` did).

## 2026-10-25 · Speech · Dictation showed no words while speaking (FIX43)
- **Symptom:** the island showed the timer and the waveform but no words; the preview showed words.
- **Root cause:** the default model (Parakeet) is an offline recognizer. `SpeechEngine::accept` only returned a `Final` when a 30-second chunk filled (`chunker.rs`) or on stop, so the native layer never emitted a `partial`. Only streaming models (Zipformer) produced partials. The preview fixture emits them for every engine.
- **Fix:** `crates/retinue-speech/src/utterance.rs` (`UtteranceTracker`) cuts audio into utterances at pauses: a partial about every 1.2 s (the whole utterance so far, spaced out when a decode is slow), a final after 0.9 s of silence, a bound of 20 s. The engine decodes on those steps (`engine.rs`). (FIX45 removed the live words and the "Words appear when you stop" line from the UI, so `useDictation` no longer consumes `onPartial`; the speech crate still emits partials. See the 2026-10-25 FIX45 entry.)
- **Avoid:** an engine that does not stream needs a segmenter to give live text; check what the real engine emits, not what the fixture emits.

## 2026-10-25 · Speech · Cactus's `libneedle.a` cannot be linked on MSVC; Whistle runs as a subprocess (FIX43)
- **Evidence:** `windows-x86_64/libneedle.a` (revision `d6c7d6a2e9655505e0f8e23339356dd1705900eb`) is one llvm-mingw COFF object (`needle.cpp.obj`) built against libc++ (`std::__1`) and the Itanium unwinder (`__gxx_personality_seh0`, `_Unwind_Resume`, `__cxa_*`), none of which the MSVC toolchain provides. The Python package loads a separate `libneedle.dll` instead.
- **Decision:** `needle.exe` from the same revision is downloaded with the model (both pinned by revision and SHA-256 in `catalog.rs`), re-hashed before every load, and run per utterance (`needle.rs`). Not verified: the engine's real output format (read leniently as JSON with `text`) and that it runs on the owner's machine; nobody executed the downloaded binary in the FIX43 lane.
- **Hash facts:** Hugging Face `?blobs=true` gives each LFS file's SHA-256 without a download; it matched a downloaded file.

## 2026-10-25 · Companion · The stick-to-bottom effect needs `mode` (FIX43)
`ui/App.tsx` follows new messages only while the log is at the bottom. Its reset branch (`chatSessionRef`) first ran when `view` changed, which used to mount the log; with the split layout the log mounts when the companion expands. Add `mode` to the effect's dependencies, or the first delivery after expanding resets the stick flag and the "New messages" pill never shows.

## 2026-10-25 · Process · Tool-call quoting, CRLF files and `vitest --root`
- Backslashes are halved in the commands the tools receive (`'\\'` arrived as `'\'`; the FIX45 lane hit it again with `node` edit scripts, so use the Edit tool for any string with `\`), and a long heredoc with apostrophes fails. Write a script file with the Write tool (use `chr(92)` for a backslash) and run it. Most `apps/desktop` and daemon files are CRLF in the working tree, `crates/retinue-storage/src/lib.rs` is LF (git marks it binary); a Python text write turns LF into CRLF and rewrites the whole file. Read and write bytes and keep each file's endings (a small `sub(path, [(old, new, count)])` helper).
- Run vitest from `apps/desktop` (`npx vitest run <file>`). `--root apps/desktop` from the repository root fails with "Denied ID ...docs/releases/...?raw" for any test that mounts `App`.
- `FIX37.updateBadge.mounted.test.tsx` expects `v0.1.0-beta.36` and fails on `main` at `30c7b3d` too (the app is beta.37); the Director decides whether to fix it. `cargo clippy --workspace` also fails in the desktop crate on Rust 1.94 in code nobody touched (`speech.rs:79`, `run_worker`'s 8 arguments, `power.rs:222`, `lib.rs` argument counts); use `--exclude retinue-desktop` for a clean run.

## 2026-10-24 · Gates · `cargo fmt --all -- --check` fails on `main` (3,766 diffs at f091ff7)
- **Symptom:** the fmt gate fails in files no lane touched (`retinue-wsl`, `retinue-speech`, `retinue-process`, `cron.rs`, `tasks.rs`, `team.rs`).
- **Root cause:** the repository is not rustfmt-formatted; it uses deliberate dense, long lines. A `git archive HEAD` copy of `f091ff7` in a temp directory reports 3,766 `Diff in` blocks, so this is the baseline.
- **Avoid:** expect the fmt gate to fail; never run `cargo fmt` to "fix" it (it rewrites thousands of lines). Keep new lines under ~100 characters where the surrounding code allows, and report the baseline count rather than claiming the gate passed.

## 2026-10-24 · Daemon tests · Debug test threads overflow the stack
- **Symptom:** `cargo test -p retinue-daemon` aborts with `STATUS_STACK_OVERFLOW` (0xc00000fd) in `fix39_p5_tests` (e.g. `row_manager_update_blob_refuses_a_locked_bot`), killing the whole binary so the rest never runs.
- **Root cause:** the default 2 MiB test-thread stack is too small for those tests' frames on this toolchain; the same tests overflow with HEAD sources restored, so it is not lane work.
- **Fix:** run daemon tests with `RUST_MIN_STACK=16777216` (`$env:RUST_MIN_STACK=16777216` in PowerShell); the suite then passes (465/465 on 2026-10-24).
- **Avoid:** if a single daemon test crashes the run without a failure line, run it alone with `--no-capture` and try `RUST_MIN_STACK` before suspecting the change.

## 2026-10-22 · Docs · Retiring a look leaves stale words in many documents
- **Symptom:** after Pop, Dot and Legacy were retired (AGENTS.md, 22 October 2026), about 40 current documents and the library still described five looks, the Flat and Dot robots as picker choices, and the pixel theme file that no longer exists.
- **Root cause:** nothing in the code gate checks prose, and the retirement changed code, tests and the handoff but not the reference pages.
- **Avoid:** when a look, art or setting is retired, sweep the current documents for its names (`grep -rniE "\bpop\b|\bdot\b|legacy|flat robot|dot robot|retinue\.css" README.md AGENTS.md docs .claude/skills`), skipping the protected and dated paths (`docs/history`, `docs/releases`, `docs/evidence`, `docs/qa-schema`, `docs/design/archive`, generated library output). Dated records keep their wording and get a one-line update note instead. Retirement dates also differ between documents (20 and 21 October 2026); AGENTS.md is the record.

## 2026-10-21 · Daemon tests · A simulated turn end must release the slot
- **Symptom:** after calling `tasks::after_turn` directly, the next `task.dispatch` for the same bot came back `queued`/`bot_busy` and versions were one bump behind.
- **Root cause:** "the bot has a live turn" is read from `active_turns`. The event loop removes the entry when a turn really ends; a test that calls the after-turn hook itself never does.
- **Avoid:** in tests, call the hook and then `st.active_turns.lock().await.remove(session)` (see `end_turn` in `main.rs` `mod task_board_tests`), or let the fake adapter emit the turn-end event.

## 2026-10-21 · UI · A handed-off UI lane drifted from the owner's reference images
- **Symptom:** the Codex UI lane's screens passed fixture screenshots and mounted tests but did not look like the owner's references. The tree was an indented list, not a node graph; the timeline bars were unlabelled; the board's main action was a native select plus Assign; the connections page had a duplicated heading and text-only cards; onboarding showed fixture chips.
- **Root cause:** the briefs described features, not the reference composition, and passing mounted tests was taken as acceptance of the UI.
- **Avoid:** put the reference image beside each UI brief and name the composition to match it. The Director compares each screen with its reference in the preview before accepting it. Fixture screenshots are not native or live evidence. Check new pages for duplicated headings and raw native controls such as `<select>`.

## 2026-10-21 · UI · A new page's segmented control was not styled in the main window
- **Symptom:** the Tasks page used the shared `segmented` class (`ui/SegmentedControl.tsx`) and needed its own styling in the main window. The visible symptom was not recorded in detail.
- **Root cause (corrected the same day; the first version named the wrong cause):** `ui/companion.css` has an unscoped `.segmented` rule, and `main.tsx` loads that sheet in the main window too. The rule reads companion-only tokens (`--companion-alpha-07`, `--c-tab-on`, `--c-dim-2`), which are defined only under `.companion-surface` and `.companion-root` (`companion.css:4`). In the main window those tokens are undefined, so the background and colours fall back to their initial values. Pop, Dot and Legacy, now removed, had styled the control under their own scopes.
- **Fix:** the Tasks page's own stylesheet adds a scoped rule, `.tasks-page .segmented` (`ui/tasks.css`, on branch `exp/board-ui`).
- **Avoid:** before using a shared class in a new page, grep for its rules (`rg -n "\.segmented" apps/desktop/src`) and check which tokens each rule reads. A token defined only under the companion scope does not apply in the main window.

## 2026-10-21 · Process · The Bash tool rejects long heredocs with apostrophes
- **Symptom:** a `cat > file <<'EOF'` heredoc that contained apostrophes failed with "unexpected EOF while looking for matching quote".
- **Root cause:** the Bash tool's parser mis-reads quotes inside long heredoc bodies.
- **Avoid:** write such files with the Write tool, or write a script file and run it. Do not rely on a long heredoc with quotes in it.

## 2026-10-21 · Verification · Browser pane screenshots can show the previous state
- **Symptom:** a screenshot showed the page before the last change, and a viewport emulation larger than the pane rendered the page scaled into one corner.
- **Root cause:** the pane captures before the new render settles, and an emulated size larger than the pane is scaled down to fit.
- **Fix:** wait about a second and take the screenshot again. Reset the emulation with the `desktop` preset and check at the pane's own size.
- **Avoid:** never judge a layout from one screenshot taken straight after a change, and never judge it from an emulated size larger than the pane.

## 2026-10-21 · Startup reads during daemon replacement
The native window could keep a startup warning after an RPC used a stopped loopback listener while the current daemon was listening elsewhere. Independent catalog checks and a clone of the development store were healthy. `daemon_rpc` now retries once on a transport/not-running error for an explicit list of observational startup methods, reacquiring the current native-held connection after `start_daemon`. Never replay `session.prompt`, settings writes, deletion, shutdown, authorization errors or invalid arguments. Native tests pin this allowlist. `runLaunchChecks` preserves string rejections from Tauri; the notice names the failed step and strips transport URLs. A rebuilt native startup was verified without the warning.

## 2026-10-20 · Owner direction · Two app themes only
Only Retinue and Stone remain. Remove retired look CSS and registrations, normalize old cached/database/backup selections to Retinue, and keep bot art independent. Do not delete saved bot-art ids as part of removing an app theme. Tests for removed themes are replaced by migration/rejection coverage. Service-worker tests must compare the precache against emitted files, not an arbitrary minimum count: deleting unused theme font chunks reduced the legitimate asset count.

## 2026-10-20 · Native links and settings events
- Calling the opener with only `opener:allow-open-url` left its URL scope empty: every valid link was rejected and the UI swallowed the error. `capabilities/main-links.json` now allows only `https://*` and `http://*`; unsafe schemes and credential-bearing URLs remain refused.
- `showMainSettings` used a renderer `emit` without emit permission. Pass the requested page through `show_main_settings` and emit it from Rust instead of broadening event permissions. Update its tray caller when changing the signature.

## 2026-10-20 · Selection, paste and development isolation
- Runtime-selection echoes must not choose a different bot. A newly selected bot may have only a local draft; preserve it and publish a session only once one exists. `App.emptyConversation.mounted.test.tsx` covers an old runtime echo and companion round trips.
- Some WebView2 image pastes have no DOM paste event. The composer also reads the native clipboard from Ctrl+V, invalidates duplicate reads when DOM image files arrive, and keeps ordinary text pastes intact. OpenCode images use advertised ACP image capability and base64 content blocks, rather than a blanket provider rejection.
- Live probes must copy the daemon executable into their own temp folder before spawning it; otherwise a concurrent Cargo build can hit Windows error 5. `live-composer.mjs` now follows the shell's private-copy pattern.

## 2026-10-19 · Process · A piped gate hid a failure before a push
- **Symptom:** the Director ran `git merge ... && node scripts/docs-library.mjs check 2>&1 | tail -1 && ... && git push`; the check failed, but `main` was pushed anyway (`917f730`).
- **Root cause:** in a pipe, the exit status is the last command's (`tail`, which succeeds), so `&&` never saw the failure.
- **Avoid:** never pipe a gate through `tail`, `head` or `Select-Object` in a chain that ends in `git push`, a release or a merge. Run each gate on its own and check its exit code (`$LASTEXITCODE` in PowerShell, `$?` or `set -o pipefail` in bash) before the next step.

## 2026-10-19 · Docs · Library discovery must use tracked files only
- **Symptom:** the owner's local scratch file `output/task-a/REPORT.md` made `node scripts/docs-library.mjs check` fail with "Document inventory has changed; rebuild the library."
- **Root cause:** discovery ran `git ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard`, so any untracked Markdown file anywhere in the checkout became a library document. The catalogue had been built without it.
- **Fix:** discovery now runs `git ls-files --cached -z` and still walks `docs/library/content/` separately for the hand-written pages. Untracked scratch files are ignored.
- **Avoid:** a documentation inventory is a tracked set plus explicit generated content, never `git ls-files --others`. When adding a source root, add it deliberately and test with an untracked Markdown file present.

## 2026-10-19 · Docs · Library hashes must ignore line endings
- **Symptom:** on a checkout with `core.autocrlf=true`, `node scripts/docs-library.mjs check` reported every text asset and snapshot stale: `scripts/docs-library.mjs`, `docs/library/assets/...`, `docs/library/content/...`.
- **Root cause:** `build` wrote hashes from the LF source files it read; `check` hashed the raw CRLF bytes produced by git checkout. Markdown snapshots had the same mismatch.
- **Fix:** `hashSource()` in `scripts/docs-library.mjs` normalises CRLF to LF for `.md`, `.css`, `.js`, `.mjs`, `.svg`, `.json` and `.html` before SHA-256; PNG stays byte-for-byte. Build, asset hashes and snapshot comparison all use it.
- **Avoid:** normalise before hashing, never hash raw working-tree bytes for text. Prove with a CRLF round-trip on one Markdown file, one CSS file and the script, then convert back to LF.
- **Churn after a build:** `npm run docs:build` rewrites every snapshot in `docs/library/sources/` with LF, so on a CRLF checkout `git status` lists ~290 modified files. Check `git diff --ignore-cr-at-eol --stat`; then `git checkout -- docs/library/sources`, which also undoes the real changes to snapshots of sources you edited; copy each edited source back over its snapshot (`docs/library/sources/<same path>`, a plain copy), and run `npm run docs:check` (it passes). Adding release notes needs a docs rebuild, or `docs:check` fails (beta.36).

## 2026-10-19 · Docs · Library guides must stay user-facing
- **Symptom:** the first library build mixed developer material into the guides: `package.json`/`Cargo.toml` version notes, `settings.set`/`appearance.style` keys, `path:line` citations and "daemon"/"adapter"/"IPC" terms; the five-minute path also repeated a stale README version note.
- **Rule (owner review):** guides (`docs/library/content/guides/*.md`, plus `overview.md` and `glossary.md`) are for people using Retinue. Remove code citations, file names, RPC/settings keys and internal terms unless the user must type or see them. Say the fact in user terms and link to the matching reference page for developer detail. Do not mention documentation discrepancies in guides.
- **Avoid:** when writing or reviewing a guide, grep for `path:line`, backticked identifiers, `daemon`, `RPC`, `adapter`, `IPC`, `settings.`, `appearance.` and `data-`; keep screenshots, captions, front blocks and working links.

## 2026-10-19 · Looks · Frost and Pop never applied in the installed app (FIX42)
- **Symptom:** beta.35 installed: Settings showed Retinue and a wallpaper selected, the window stayed plain dark; the browser preview looked right.
- **Root cause:** `loadStyle` in `apps/desktop/src/blob/retinue.ts` picked the look stylesheet with a nested ternary of three CSS-only `import()` calls. Vite's production build turned it into one `__vitePreload(..., __vite__mapDeps([7]))` whose dependency list was only the last branch (`dot.css`), so Frost and Pop loaded Dot's CSS. Proven over the WebView2 debugging port (loaded sheets: `index`, `inter`, `dot`) and in the built JS. The dev server does not rewrite preloads, so the preview hid it.
- **Fix:** all look stylesheets are static imports in `apps/desktop/src/main.tsx`; `src/ui/themeBuild.test.ts` forbids dynamic imports of look CSS (`098831e`, merged in `c119f93`, shipped in v0.1.0-beta.36). Since 20 October 2026 the only look stylesheet is `ui/themes/frost.css`; the Dot and Pop sheets named above were removed in `5eb6879`.
- **Avoid:** never put several `import()` calls in one conditional expression; give each its own arrow function (as `ui/fonts.ts` does). Check bundling-sensitive changes in `npm run build` output or the installed app, not only the preview.

## 2026-10-19 · Process · A lane's root cause can be wrong while its fix works
FIX42's lane blamed "lazy CSS does not load in the WebView" (contradicted by Legacy's lazy CSS working for many betas). Always test the stated cause against evidence before recording it.

## 2026-10-19 · Toolchain · Rust 1.94 on the build machine
`AtomicU64::try_update` is unstable on 1.94 (fixed with `fetch_update`, `28da243`); clippy on 1.94 flags `!x.is_some_and(...)` patterns (use `is_none_or`), needless borrows and items after test modules (`7412a9c`). Earlier betas were built with a newer toolchain. Run clippy before every release.

## 2026-10-19 · Native build · Apostrophe in the checkout path
`cargo check/test/build -p retinue-desktop` fails with `RC2135 file not found: D:\Basem\'s\...\retinue.ico` from the main checkout. Build the desktop crate and releases from a short path (`D:\fxNN`, `D:\bNN`).

## 2026-10-19 · Lanes · OpenCode 2 flags
OpenCode 2.0 removed `--variant` and `--dir`; effort is `-m provider/model#high`, run from the worktree, add `--auto`. Old flags print help and exit 1. Upgrading OpenCode while a lane runs killed two runs (exit code 9).

## 2026-10-19 · Lanes · Codex sandbox limits
No network, no writes outside the workspace: npm installs fail, and Vite/Vitest cannot write their temp config through a `node_modules` junction (`EPERM`; use `--configLoader runner`). The Director installs packages and runs the full gates.

## 2026-10-19 · Tests · Load-induced failures
Full Vitest runs alongside cargo builds or lanes produced 57 and 100 false failures (timeouts). Re-run failing files alone before treating them as real.

## 2026-10-19 · Tests · Settings broadcast queue
A daemon test that sets many settings must assert the broadcast events it cares about before setting more keys: every `settings.set` queues a `settings.changed` event, and later `events.recv()` calls read the queued ones first (FIX40 `appearance_settings_validate_persist_and_broadcast`).

## 2026-10-19 · Design · Selected rows in filled looks
Dot and Pop were removed on 20 October 2026, but the rule stands for any filled look. Dot (inverted) and Pop (butter) selections both shipped with unreadable descendants at first (white title on white; cream role chip and amber status on butter) because base rules for `.role-chip`, row times and `.bot-row-preview.waiting` are more specific. Scope the overrides under the selected element with enough specificity and check every descendant in dark and light.

## 2026-10-19 · Design · Frost glass level
At tint .84 the Lake wallpaper's bright band bled through all panels as a stripe; Solid is tint .93 / blur 24 px. The owner wants Solid as the default and no glow anywhere.

## 2026-10-19 · CSS · Mini avatars
`ArtFrame` marks small avatars with `data-mini`, not a class; style them with `.art-frame[data-mini] ...`.

## 2026-10-19 · Daemon · Look and settings keys are allowlisted
`valid_look` rejects unknown top-level look keys, and `normalizeLook`, `agentLook`, `lookKey` rebuild looks from known keys only: a new look field must be added in both TypeScript and `crates/retinue-storage/src/lib.rs`, or it is silently dropped or rejected. Haze's `icon` key (null or one of `logo`, `logo-tint`, `logo-mono`, `px-robot` … `px-bolt`, `ICONS` in `valid_look`) is the latest example: it also needs the Manager descriptions in `team.rs` and `proposals.rs`.

## 2026-10-19 · Links · Safe opener
Chat links are `<a href target="_blank">` routed through `openSafeLink`; middle-click and Ctrl/Cmd/Shift-click must also go through it (`onAuxClick`), and the desktop app must never fall back to `window.open` inside the webview.

## 2026-10-19 · Release · SHA256SUMS
`scripts/release-windows.ps1` does not create `SHA256SUMS.txt`; upload it separately (format `<sha256>  <installer>` and a newline) and verify its digest.

## 2026-10-24 · Preview · Portals into a React-owned node
Portaling tooltips and drag ghosts into the page's root `.mini` element (rendered by React) threw `removeChild ... not a child of this node` on unmount. Portal into an empty host element that React renders but never fills (`<div id="mini-portal" />`) and keep it inside the themed root so its CSS variables still apply. A page that is both a Vite entry and edited live also re-runs `createRoot` on hot reload; keep the root on the container (`host.miniRoot ??= createRoot(host)`).

## 2026-10-24 · CSS · Lifting content above a background layer broke absolutely placed children
- **Symptom:** in Haze the side-conversation pill shrank to 23 px around one logo, and the companion focus card's avatar slid onto the name.
- **Root cause:** `.chat-header > :not(.haze-aura) { position: relative; z-index: 1 }` (and the same on `.island-card.focus`) made `.chat-title` the containing block of the absolutely centred `.side-header`, and overrode `.card-bot`'s `position: absolute`.
- **Fix:** keep the children as they are; give the parent `isolation: isolate` and the background layer `z-index: -1`.
- **Avoid:** never re-position a component's children from a theme to stack them; push the decoration down instead. Likewise, a theme token used as a page background (`--surface-panel`, `--surface-app`) is also used by popovers and ring cut-outs: making it transparent made the profile menu see-through and the usage rings pies (rings now cut their centre with a mask).

## 2026-10-25 · Composer · Model confirmation and effort / attachment geometry
- **Model confirmation (FIX44):** the dialog is now tied to real context loss, not to “any changed model”. `main.rs:update_session_model` requires `confirmModelChange` only when the conversation has user messages, a delivered provider context (`session_had_provider_turns`) or a provider session on record (`providerSessionId`, `contextUsed`, `contextModel`); otherwise a first choice in an empty chat applies silently. The composer mirrors this in `modelChangeNeedsWarning` (`SessionExecutionControls.tsx`) and also reacts to a `confirmation_required` refusal by showing the same dialog, so the daemon contract still holds. A model change now writes `session.model.update` **before** `agent.update`, so cancelling leaves both the conversation and the bot untouched; effort and speed changes keep the old bot-first order that the open picker relies on.
- **Geometry (FIX44):** `.composer-attachment-remove` is a 24px box with `padding: 0`, `place-items: center` and a `--focus-ring` on `:focus-visible`. `.session-effort-popover` is `width: max-content` with `min-width: min(320px, calc(100vw - 24px))` and `max-width: min(460px, calc(100vw - 24px))`; labels are `min-width: max-content` in a wrapping flex row, so they are never ellipsized and a narrow window wraps them. The old 320/404px fixed widths and the ellipsis on `.session-effort-value` are gone.
- **Sent chat images (FIX44):** `session.prompt` copies each accepted image into `<data dir>/message-images/<turn>-<n>.<ext>` and records `file` on the message (older messages keep only `path`). The new `message.image` RPC takes `{sessionId, messageId, index}` — never a path — checks the message belongs to the conversation, re-checks the image signature, and returns a data URL; a missing/damaged file is `not_found`, which the UI renders as a placeholder. Deleting a conversation removes its copied files. `MessageAttachments` shows thumbnails with a viewer (Escape/backdrop close, focus returns); without a session/message identity it falls back to the old filename chip.
- **Steer queue (FIX44):** the queue banner and the steer/queue line moved inside `.composer-box` as `ComposerStatus` (`PromptQueue.tsx`): one 22px row that always exists, a count chip that unfolds the rows, and paused/retry/queue-reason text in that same row. Do not put the queue banner back outside the card; it stacks and crowds the composer.
- **Saved chats and titles (FIX44):** `experiments::title_from_prompt` now strips fenced code and code-looking first lines, drops leading greetings/openers ("hey, can you fix the login bug" → "fix the login bug"), and falls back to `Shared N images`, `Code review`, `Quick hello` or `Quick chat` for prompts that name nothing. The storage write still refuses a renamed row, a side conversation and any conversation with a prior user message.
- **Permission reversion (FIX44):** `permissionWrite` (`ComposerControls.tsx`) writes `null` only when the choice *is* the bot's default, and **always** writes `bypass` for Full access. Writing `null` for bypass cleared the bot's setting whenever the bot already defaulted to bypass, so the conversation fell back to the global Ask. A model lock's permission pin still applies from the next turn only. Separately, the snapshot's agents (`Storage::snapshot` → `agent_rows_locked`) did not select `approval_mode`, so after a reload the composer showed the global default even though the bot was bypass; the snapshot now carries `approvalMode` and `effectiveApprovalMode`. The UI never calls `permissions.policy.get`, so the snapshot is the only source.
- **A reload mounts before the daemon answers.** `message.image` is now in `retry_startup_read` (`src-tauri/src/lib.rs`), and `MessageImage` retries a "daemon is not running" refusal (not a `not_found`) before showing its placeholder. Any new read RPC that the transcript calls on mount needs both, or a reload shows a permanent placeholder.
- **Avoid:** when diagnosing a “new chat” warning, check the session id/persisted-vs-draft state and message count separately. For picker geometry, inspect computed button padding as well as grid alignment.

## 2026-10-26 · Task dragging and details

The board's internal task drag must use pointer capture, a movement threshold and elementFromPoint hit-testing against data-agent-id lanes. WebView2 native file drag handling can intercept the HTML drag path; do not disable native file drops globally to fix an internal board gesture. A drop still uses the existing connected/busy checks and daemon dispatch; suppress the task-open click only after a drag. Plain clicks and the Assign menu remain available.

Task detail dialogs close on a direct backdrop pointer-down. Only the detail body scrolls; header/actions stay visible. Render brief/goal/report via SafeMarkdown and openSafeLink rather than raw paragraphs. Keep Retry left, Close/Open conversation and state actions right, with matching control radii. The tree/timeline structure is unchanged. Owner requested rapid edits without a test sweep.

## 2026-10-26 · Native · A see-through look needs a transparent native window, not only transparent CSS (Haze, uncommitted)

Symptom: Haze's background could not be made transparent in CSS alone. Cause: the main window was created without `transparent` (`tauri.conf.json`), so WebView2 painted an opaque default background behind the page. Fix: `"transparent": true` on the main window (tao then applies the same empty DWM blur region as the companion window, wry sets the WebView2 default background to alpha 0), plus an opaque base for the looks that must not be see-through (Frost body colour under its wallpaper; Stone's `--surface-app` is already opaque). It is a creation-time property: a running window needs a rebuild and restart. Not verified in a running native window (the owner forbade tests and screenshots in that wave).

## 2026-10-26 (T3 wave)

- **A second dev app must not share the identifier.** `tauri-plugin-single-instance` is keyed by the bundle identifier, so `scripts/dev-windows.ps1`'s `com.retinue.desktop.dev` can fold into an already running review build. The T3 preview uses `com.retinue.desktop.t3dev` and Vite ports 1440 to 1459, with its own `RETINUE_DB_PATH`.
- **Media and CSP.** `img-src` already allowed `data:`/`blob:`; audio and video needed `media-src 'self' blob:`. `fetch(dataUrl)` is blocked (`connect-src` has no `data:`), so decode base64 yourself into a Blob. WebView2's PDF viewer does not run in our CSP or inside a sandboxed iframe, so PDFs are a card, not an inline view.
- **A delegation turn can end before its row is "running".** `team::after_turn` runs in its own task, so a fast turn can finish before `delegations::started`; `started` must read the turn's stored state back and finish the row itself.
- **Rich tables must keep the author's text.** Converting "12%" or "$1,200" to a number for display drops the unit; keep the cell text and read the number only to sort or plot.
- **Heredocs and regex backslashes.** Writing a Node patch script through an unquoted shell heredoc strips backslashes in regexes (`\s` becomes `s`); write the script with the file tool, then run it.
- **node_modules is shared through the worktree junctions.** Adding a package: run `npm install <pkg> --workspace @retinue/desktop --package-lock-only` in one worktree, then put the package into the main `node_modules` (or run `npm install` in main); the lanes' junctions see it at once. A lane worktree the Director created may have **no** junction at all (17 November 2026: `tsc` not found), and `git status` will not say so: `cmd /c mklink /J "<lane>\node_modules" "<any lane>\node_modules"` makes one in a second, and `npm run typecheck` and `npx vitest run` then work.
- **Two spawn sites can close a future-type cycle (E0391).** A request hook in `forward_events` that awaited `deliver` made `session_prompt_impl` depend on itself; a synchronous `spawn_update` (a plain `fn` that spawns) breaks the cycle. Spawning an `async` block that awaits the delivery path does not.
- **Do not type-check tests when the owner says no tests.** `apps/desktop/tsconfig.json` includes the test files, so `tsc -p apps/desktop`, `tsc -b` and `npm run build` all compile them. For a no-tests wave use a temporary tsconfig that extends it and excludes `*.test.ts(x)` (`apps/desktop/tsconfig.prod.tmp.json`, untracked), and `npx vite build` for the bundle.
- **A PDF viewer needs three things in a Tauri CSP.** The worker must be your own bundled file (`...worker.min.mjs?url`), the viewer must draw to a canvas (no iframe), and `isEvalSupported` must be false. WebView2's built-in PDF viewer is not available in our setup.
- **A question holds its assignment open.** A side conversation's turn that ends with a pending `ask_user` card is not a result: `delegations::after_turn` returns `TurnEnd::Waiting` (no finish, no reply claim, no relay) and the answer's turn is adopted by the same running row. Anything that finishes a side assignment (cancel, fail, interrupt, restart) must also close that session's pending cards without prompting the child. The side-conversation gate in `session_prompt_impl` has to allow `PromptOrigin::QuestionAnswer`, or the answer is refused `read_only` and pauses the queue.

## 2026-10-27 · Native · Haze frosted glass needs the OS compositor; CSS cannot blur the desktop (branch haze/glass, uncommitted)

Symptom/ask: "Haze should be frosted glass", but the 26 October fix only made the page 80% opaque over a transparent window, which shows the desktop sharp, not blurred. Cause: WebView2 composes only its own page, so `backdrop-filter` can never blur what is behind the window; only DWM can. Fix: `src-tauri/src/backdrop.rs` calls `window-vibrancy` directly (Tauri's `set_effects` swallows errors, so it cannot report a refusal): build 22523+ `DWMSBT_TRANSIENTWINDOW` (tint colour ignored), 17763+ composition acrylic with a tint, then `apply_blur`. `ui/windowBackdrop.ts` watches `data-style` and `data-theme` and sets `<html data-backdrop>` only when the blur is really drawn, and `haze.css` then turns `.haze-backdrop` into a thin veil plus the blobs over it (never an opaque layer); without it the opaque 80% backdrop remains the fallback. Details that bit or could bite: the system acrylic follows the window's immersive dark mode, so the shell forces `set_theme(Dark|Light)` to match the app and `set_theme(None)` when leaving Haze (window-level `set_theme` only touches the title bar, not WebView2's colour scheme); Windows' Transparency effects switch makes acrylic a flat colour while the call still succeeds, so read the `EnableTransparency` value under `HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Themes\Personalize`; first paint needs both `backdrop.json` re-applied in `setup` and `retinue.backdrop` in localStorage read by `index.html`. A fresh worktree's `cargo check -p retinue-desktop` needs the gitignored sidecars in `src-tauri/binaries/` and `apps/desktop/dist/` (`npm run build -w apps/desktop`); `--lib` avoids neither. **Not verified at runtime** (owner rule: no screenshots or tests): that the transparent decorated WebView2 window shows the backdrop, the density, and Windows 10 resize lag. Isolated dev window: `D:\hz1\target\lane\run-hzdev.ps1` (identifier `com.retinue.desktop.hzglass`, ports 1470 to 1489).

## 2026-10-31 · Machines · What Phase 4 taught

- **Compare host names without regard to case.** Native's certificate names the machine by `COMPUTERNAME`, which is upper case, and every client sends it lower case, so a link by host name was refused with `forbidden_origin` until `web.rs` used `eq_ignore_ascii_case`.
- **`st.emit` persists events.** Anything that must stay in memory can't ride in an event payload. `machine.changed` first carried the other machine's bot names into the event log; it now carries only `{machineId, state}`.
- **A value read once goes stale when the other side owns it.** The link's tier was only read at pairing, so raising it on the other computer never unlocked Take over. `refresh_one` now reads `GET /api/me`.
- **A read that fails is not evidence.** `repo_identity::Origin::Unknown` keeps the stored identity. Only a finished git run clears it. Use the same shape for any derived column.
- **A security-relevant field needs one writer.** `trustedSharedHash` could be set through `project.update`, skipping the confirm step. The public validator now refuses it, a private one reads stored rows, and one storage method writes it.
- **`std::fs::write` follows a planted link.** A repository can hold a symlink at a temp path you're about to write. Use `create_new`, and on `AlreadyExists` remove that path and retry once.
- **`FETCH_HEAD` is per worktree.** A fast-forward inside a linked worktree must name the fetched commit by id.
- **Any `Command::output()` on a network command needs a real limit.** A fetch can hang. `worktree_attach::run_limited` polls `try_wait`, reads stdout on its own thread, and kills the tree with `taskkill /T` at the limit.
- **Never let an import overwrite a live row.** An id from another machine can match a conversation here, for example after copying a database. Only a row whose `movedTo` names the giving machine may be updated.
- **A dialog opened from inside a dialog needs a portal.** `.blob-dialog` has a transform and a `backdrop-filter`, so a nested fixed backdrop is trapped inside it. `TakeOverDialog` uses `createPortal`.
- **A long reading surface can't be Haze glass.** `--surface-overlay` is translucent, and text behind a transcript collides with it. `.machine-view` mixes it with `Canvas`.

## 2026-10-31 · Phases 5 to 7 · What they taught

- **A per-bot environment is also a process key.** `options.env` is hashed into the shared-process key by the Codex and ACP adapters, and Claude spawns per session. So a per-bot variable must join `ExecOptions.env` at `main.rs`, not the child's inherited environment, or two bots silently share the first bot's process.
- **A provider's ACP flag isn't always the `acp` subcommand.** Gemini CLI and Qwen Code take `--acp`. `adapter_for` returns `None` for an unknown provider, so a provider that's listed but runs nothing fails there: check it first.
- **`codex app-server generate-json-schema` needs `--experimental`** to match what the adapter negotiates. `core.autocrlf` rewrites pinned JSON on checkout, hence `schema/.gitattributes`.
- **Who is signed in:** `claude auth status` is JSON. `codex login status` only says "Logged in using ChatGPT", so use the app server's `account/read`, skipping lines with no `id`. `opencode auth list` names no person, and its `label` is the credential kind. Never run `opencode auth export`: it prints secrets.
- **Web Push:** the `aes128gcm` additional data is empty (RFC 8188 section 2.1), not the header. `hkdf`'s `expand` appends the `0x01` counter itself. `serde_json::json!` sorts keys, so build VAPID segments by hand.
- **`.retinue.json` already had a field.** `instructions` shipped in beta.43, so new code must keep unknown keys when writing and accept a file with no `version`. Read the release notes for a file's history before writing a format for it.
- **OpenCode lanes share a process.** When one turn hit the installed app's 30-minute limit, the other OpenCode turns ended seconds later, with no error of their own. Their files were fine. Keep OpenCode turns short on any build before beta.44, and resend to all of them.
- **The app runs four bot conversations at once** (`concurrency.botsAtOnce`, 1 to 16, the owner's setting). A send over that returns an error saying it will start later, and it does not start. Check a lane really started.
- **A release script must stop on a failed test.** `release_b44` carried on after UI timeouts under load. They were harmless, but the script couldn't know that.
- **Tests that make real requests need a limit that survives a busy machine.** The everyday 10-second client limit failed handoff tests while lanes compiled. Use a long limit in tests, and don't count events exactly while a background loop runs.

## 2026-11-01 · Usage · A `quota.updated` event with no snapshot loses its reason

- **Symptom:** Claude and OpenCode Go read "Unavailable" in the usage panel while Codex shows its meters, and the panel offers no way to turn reading on.
- **Cause:** the daemon's row is honest (`snapshot: null`, `lastError: "read_off"`), but `applyEvent` in `apps/desktop/src/types.ts` builds the fallback row for an event whose `quota.snapshot` is not an object, and that fallback hard-codes `lastError: null` with `snapshot: quota`. `quotaModel.ts` then has no windows and no error, so it says `Unavailable`, and `QuotaBar.tsx`'s "Turn on" (which needs `readOff`) never renders. Codex is fine because its snapshot is an object and takes the other branch.
- **Why it hides:** `quota.list` is fetched once per connection (`App.tsx`), and the honest row lives only milliseconds; every poll (5 minutes, `runtime.changed`, and each `read_off` persist) rewrites it through the event path.
- **Check the DB, not the UI:** `quota_snapshots` in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Retinue\retinue.db` holds `last_error` per provider (`read_off`, `no_credentials`, `unavailable`, …). A missing `usage.readCliSignIns` row means the owner never allowed the read, which is the default.
- **Rule:** an event reducer must carry every field the list row carries. When a payload nests a nullable snapshot, keep `lastError`, `failureCount` and `retryAfter` rather than inventing a fresh row.

## 2026-11-03: opening a conversation showed "now" (updated_at bumped by housekeeping)

- **Symptom:** clicking a conversation made its row read "now" though nothing was sent.
- **Cause:** opening warms the provider (`session.warm` → resume in `main.rs`). The resume called `update_session_state(.., "idle")` (and `clear_session_provider_id`), and both write `updated_at`, which the sidebar shows as the conversation's age.
- **Fix:** `Storage::set_session_state_quietly` (no `updated_at`) for the resume path; `clear_session_provider_id` no longer touches `updated_at`.
- **Avoid:** `updated_at` means "something happened in the conversation". Housekeeping (resume, clearing a provider id) must not write it.

## 2026-11-08 · Native (GPUI) fix round 2 · The window quit without a word

- **A panic in a GPUI window looks like a silent quit.** `native.log` ended with "daemon child stopped" and no "exited": the panic unwound through `Root`'s drop (stopping the daemon), then crossed an `extern "C"` boundary and aborted, so `main` never reached its last log line and the message went to a stderr nobody watches. Install a panic hook as the **first** thing in `main` (`trace::install_panic_hook`): it writes `PANIC at <file>:<line>:<col>: <message>` and a `std::backtrace::Backtrace::force_capture()` (which ignores `RUST_BACKTRACE`, so a debug build always has frames).
- **The cause was a GPUI double lease:** "cannot update Root while it is already being updated". `Entity::lease` *removes* the entity from the map while it is leased, so `entity.update`/`entity.read` on the same entity from inside its own listener or update panics, and the panic aborts the process.
